### World Information Placeholders

Source: https://github.com/placeholderapi/placeholderapi/wiki/Placeholders

Provides a wide range of PlaceholderAPI placeholders for retrieving various details about a Minecraft world, such as biome, entity counts, time, game rules, player statistics, and economy balance (requires an economy plugin like EssentialsX for total balance).

```APIDOC
%world_biome%
  - Displays the biome of the player's current location.
  - Returns: String (biome name).

%world_nearbyEntities_<radius>%
  - Displays the number of entities within a specified radius around the player.
  - Parameters:
    - <radius>: The radius in blocks.
  - Returns: Integer (count).

%world_total%
  - Displays the total number of players online across all worlds.
  - Returns: Integer (count).

%world_name_<world>%
  - Displays the name of a specified world.
  - Parameters:
    - <world>: The name of the world.
  - Returns: String (world name).

%world_seed_<world>%
  - Displays the seed of a specified world.
  - Parameters:
    - <world>: The name of the world.
  - Returns: Long (world seed).

%world_sealevel_<world>%
  - Displays the sea level of a specified world.
  - Parameters:
    - <world>: The name of the world.
  - Returns: Integer (sea level).

%world_time_<world>%
  - Displays the current time in a specified world (24-hour format).
  - Parameters:
    - <world>: The name of the world.
  - Returns: String (time).

%world_timein12_<world>%
  - Displays the current time in a specified world (12-hour format with AM/PM).
  - Parameters:
    - <world>: The name of the world.
  - Returns: String (time).

%world_canpvp_<world>%
  - Checks if PvP is enabled in a specified world.
  - Parameters:
    - <world>: The name of the world.
  - Returns: Boolean (true/false).

%world_thunder_<world>%
  - Checks if it is thundering in a specified world.
  - Parameters:
    - <world>: The name of the world.
  - Returns: Boolean (true/false).

%world_animalallowed_<world>%
  - Checks if animal spawning is allowed in a specified world.
  - Parameters:
    - <world>: The name of the world.
  - Returns: Boolean (true/false).

%world_monsterallowed_<world>%
  - Checks if monster spawning is allowed in a specified world.
  - Parameters:
    - <world>: The name of the world.
  - Returns: Boolean (true/false).

%world_difficulty_<world>%
  - Displays the difficulty setting of a specified world.
  - Parameters:
    - <world>: The name of the world.
  - Returns: String (difficulty name).

%world_players_<world>%
  - Displays the number of players currently in a specified world.
  - Parameters:
    - <world>: The name of the world.
  - Returns: Integer (count).

%world_players_<group>_<world>%
  - Displays the number of players in a specified group within a specified world.
  - Parameters:
    - <group>: The player group (e.g., 'op', 'member').
    - <world>: The name of the world.
  - Returns: Integer (count).

%world_haspermission_<permission>_<world>%
  - Checks if a specified world has a given permission.
  - Parameters:
    - <permission>: The permission node.
    - <world>: The name of the world.
  - Returns: Boolean (true/false).

%world_playerexist_<player>_<world>%
  - Checks if a specified player exists in a specified world.
  - Parameters:
    - <player>: The player's name.
    - <world>: The name of the world.
  - Returns: Boolean (true/false).

%world_isgamerule_<gamerule>_<world>%
  - Checks the status of a specified gamerule in a specified world.
  - Parameters:
    - <gamerule>: The gamerule name (e.g., 'doDaylightCycle').
    - <world>: The name of the world.
  - Returns: Boolean (true/false) or String (value).

%world_recentjoin_<world>%
  - Displays the name of the most recent player to join a specified world.
  - Parameters:
    - <world>: The name of the world.
  - Returns: String (player name).

%world_recentquit_<world>%
  - Displays the name of the most recent player to quit a specified world.
  - Parameters:
    - <world>: The name of the world.
  - Returns: String (player name).

%world_totalbalance_<world>%
  - Displays the total economy balance of all players in a specified world. Requires an economy plugin like EssentialsX.
  - Parameters:
    - <world>: The name of the world.
  - Returns: Double (total balance).
```

It includes options for prefixes, rainbow prefixes, top player statistics, specific player statistics, current player statistics, and server-wide statistics, with filtering capabilities for item/block, entity, and raw number values.

```PlaceholderAPI
%playerstats_server,<statistic>%

%playerstats_server,<statistic:entity>%
%playerstats_server,<statistic>,only:number>%
%playerstats_server,<statistic>,only:number_raw>%
%playerstats_title,<statistic>%
```

### BasicQuests Placeholders

Source: https://github.com/placeholderapi/placeholderapi/wiki/temp_wiki_06579f44c797ae903e1c8d72358bb1c9bdeca778/Placeholders

Placeholders for the BasicQuests plugin, providing comprehensive information about a player's quests. This includes formatted multi-line lists of quests and rewards, total quest count, and detailed information for individual quests at a given index, such as title, progress, remaining actions, and formatted lines suitable for signs and scoreboards.

```PlaceholderAPI
%bquests% - (formatted, multiline) list of a players quests
%bquests_detail% - (formatted, multiline) list of a players quests and rewards
%bquests_count% - Amount of quests a player has
%bquests_<index>% - Formatted quest at the given index
%bquests_<index>_title% - Title of the quest at the given index
%bquests_<index>_progress% - Progress of the quest at the given index
%bquests_<index>_left% - Amount of (actions) left to complete the quest at the given index
%bquests_<index>_<linenumber>% - Title of a given quest split into 4 lines that fit on a sign.
%bquests_<index>_reward_<linenumber>% - Reward of a given quest split into 2 lines. Handy for Scoreboards
```

### PlaceholderAPI Plugin Information Expansion

Source: https://github.com/placeholderapi/placeholderapi/wiki/Placeholders

Retrieves information about the status and existence of specified Minecraft plugins. Useful for conditional displays or debugging plugin states.

```APIDOC
Download Command:
/papi ecloud download Plugin

Usage:
%plugin_isenabled_<plugin name>%
  - Returns true if the specified plugin is enabled, false otherwise.
%plugin_isdisabled_<plugin name>%
  - Returns true if the specified plugin is disabled, false otherwise.
%plugin_exists_<plugin name>%
  - Returns true if the specified plugin exists on the server, false otherwise.
```

### MBedwars Current Match Stats Placeholders

Source: https://github.com/placeholderapi/placeholderapi/wiki/Placeholders

Placeholders for a player's statistics within the current MBedwars match, including kills, final kills, deaths, kill/death ratio, beds destroyed, and play time. Custom game statistics can be added using the general format %mbedwars_gamestats-<id>%.


```APIDOC
%mbedwars_gamestats-kills%
%mbedwars_gamestats-final_kills%
%mbedwars_gamestats-deaths%
%mbedwars_gamestats-kd%
%mbedwars_gamestats-beds_destroyed%
%mbedwars_gamestats-play_time%
```

--------------------------------

### ChatReaction Plugin Placeholders

Source: https://github.com/placeholderapi/placeholderapi/wiki/Placeholders

Placeholders for the ChatReaction plugin, providing statistics, game status, and winner information for chat reaction events. Some statistics require MySQL.

```APIDOC
%chatreaction_wins% - Chat reaction wins (Requires MySQL)
%chatreaction_wins_<playername>% - Chat reactions wins for a certain player (Requires MySQL)
%chatreaction_top_player_<placement>% - Returns the player at the placement inputted from the leaderboard (Requires MySQL)
%chatreaction_top_wins_<placement>% - Returns the amount of wins a player has at that placement (Requires MySQL)
%chatreaction_type% - Type of reaction (Reaction or Scramble)
%chatreaction_active_round% - Is a round currently active
%chatreaction_display_word% - The scrambled word
%chatreaction_reaction_word% - The actual solution word
%chatreaction_start_time% - Time when the round started
%chatreaction_latest_winner% - Last recorded winner
%chatreaction_start_time_in_seconds% - Returns the time (in seconds) since the chat reaction started
%chatreaction_time_remaining% - returns the time (in seconds) when the chat reaction event will expire
```

### Custom Advancements Plugin Placeholders

Source: https://github.com/placeholderapi/placeholderapi/wiki/Placeholders

PlaceholderAPI placeholders for the Custom Advancements plugin, displaying progress and status of player advancements, including specific trees and paths.

```PlaceholderAPI
%customadvancements_available_advancements% - Displays the number of available advancements for the player
%customadvancements_available_advancements_<tree>% - Displays the number of available advancements for a specific advancement tree
%customadvancements_completed_advancements% - Displays the number of completed advancements.
%customadvancements_completed_advancements_<tree>% - Displays the number of completed advancements for a specific advancement tree.
%customadvancements_active_advancements% - Displays the number of active advancements.
%customadvancements_active_advancements_<tree>% - Displays the number of active advancements for a specific advancement tree.
%customadvancements_progress_percentage_<path>% - Displays the progression in percentages for a specific path (e.g. example.advancement1).
%customadvancements_progress_<path>% - Displays the progress made for a specific advancement path.
%customadvancements_max_progress_<path>% - Displays the maximum progress for a specific advancement path.
%customadvancements_meet_requirements_<path>% - Displays either true or false depending if the player meets all requirements for a specific advancement path.
```

### Seasons Plugin Placeholders

Source: https://github.com/placeholderapi/placeholderapi/wiki/temp_wiki_06579f44c797ae903e1c8d72358bb1c9bdeca778/Placeholders

Placeholders for the Seasons plugin, providing information about the current season, weather, and day. These can be used globally or for a specific world by replacing `[world]` with the world's name. No direct download command is provided.

```APIDOC
%seasons_season%
%seasons_season_[world]%
%seasons_weather%
%seasons_weather_[world]%
%seasons_day%
%seasons_day_[world]%

Replace [world] with the name of a loaded world.
Placeholders ending in a [world] will retrieve information from the specified world instead of the player's current world.
```

### PlaceholderAPI RelCon Expansion

Source: https://github.com/placeholderapi/placeholderapi/wiki/Placeholders

Allows for conditional text display based on player or viewer permissions. This is useful for creating dynamic content that changes based on a user's access levels.

```APIDOC
Download Command:
/papi ecloud download RelCon

Usage:
%rel_relcon_player_<permission>_<text1>_[text2]%
  - If the player has <permission>, displays <text1>. Optionally, if they don't, displays [text2].
%rel_relcon_player_<permission>_<text1>%
  - If the player has <permission>, displays <text1>.
%rel_relcon_viewer_<permission>_<text1>_[text2]%
  - If the viewer has <permission>, displays <text1>. Optionally, if they don't, displays [text2].
%rel_relcon_viewer_<permission>_<text1>%
  - If the viewer has <permission>, displays <text1>.
```

### PlaceholderAPI ParseNear Expansion Placeholders

Source: https://github.com/placeholderapi/placeholderapi/wiki/temp_wiki_06579f44c797ae903e1c8d72358bb1c9bdeca778/Placeholders

Documents the ParseNear expansion for PlaceholderAPI, which allows parsing any placeholder for the closest player. It supports parsing within a specified radius and returns blank if no player is found within the scope.

```APIDOC
%parsenear_<placeholder_without_percent_signs>%          # Parses placeholder for closest player
%parsenear_<radius>_<placeholder_without_percent_signs>% # Parses placeholder for closest player in a radius
```

### Import PlaceholderAPI Dependency with Maven

Source: https://github.com/placeholderapi/placeholderapi/wiki/Hook-into-PlaceholderAPI

Add the PlaceholderAPI repository and dependency to your "pom.xml" file for Maven projects. Replace "{VERSION}" with the specific PlaceholderAPI version you intend to use.

```XML
    <repositories>
        <repository>
            <id>placeholderapi</id>
            <url>https://repo.extendedclip.com/content/repositories/placeholderapi/</url>
        </repository>
    </repositories>
    <dependencies>
        <dependency>
         <groupId>me.clip</groupId>
          <artifactId>placeholderapi</artifactId>
          <version>{VERSION}</version>
         <scope>provided</scope>
        </dependency>
    </dependencies>
```

### PlaceholderAPI Animations Expansion

Source: https://github.com/placeholderapi/placeholderapi/wiki/temp_wiki_06579f44c797ae903e1c8d72358bb1c9bdeca778/Placeholders

Allows dynamic text animations using specific HTML-like tags. It's crucial to note that when using other placeholders within the animation text, they must be enclosed in curly brackets (e.g., {player_name}) instead of percentage signs.

```HTML
%animations_<tag>Text</tag>%
%animations_<tag option>Text</tag>%
%animations_<tag option=:value>Text</tag>%
```

### Implement Relational PlaceholderAPI Expansion in Java

Source: https://github.com/placeholderapi/placeholderapi/wiki/PlaceholderExpansion

This Java code defines a `PlaceholderExpansion` that implements the `Relational` interface for PlaceholderAPI. It demonstrates how to create a custom relational placeholder (`%rel_example_friend%`) that checks if two players are friends using an external `SomePlugin` instance. The `onPlaceholderRequest` method handles the logic for the placeholder, returning a formatted string based on the friendship status and requiring both players to be online.

```java
package at.helpch.placeholderapi.example.expansions;

import at.helpch.placeholderapi.example.SomePlugin;
import org.bukkit.ChatColor;
import org.bukkit.Player;
import me.clip.placeholderapi.expansion.PlaceholderExpansion;
import me.clip.placeholderapi.expansion.Relational;

public class SomeExpansion extends PlaceholderExpansion implements Relational {

    private final SomePlugin plugin;
    
    public SomeExpansion(SomePlugin plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
    }
    
    @Override
    public String getAuthor() {
        return "someauthor";
    }
    
    @Override
    public String getIdentifier() {
        return "example";
    }

    @Override
    public String getVersion() {
        return "1.0.0";
    }
    
    @Override
    public boolean persist() {
        return true; // This is required or else PlaceholderAPI will unregister the Expansion on reload
    }
    
    @Override
    public String onPlaceholderRequest(Player one, Player two, String identifier) {
        if(one == null || two == null)
            return null; // We require both Players to be online
            
        if(identifier.equalsIgnoreCase("friend")) {
            if(plugin.areFriends(one, two)) {
                return ChatColor.GREEN + one.getName() + " and " + two.getName() + " are friends!";
            } else {
                return ChatColor.GREEN + one.getName() + " and " + two.getName() + " are not friends!";
            }
        }
        
        return null; // Placeholder is unknown by the Expansion
    }
}
```

### PlaceholderAPI ChangeOutput Expansion Placeholders

Source: https://github.com/placeholderapi/placeholderapi/wiki/Placeholders

Modifies placeholder output based on conditional matching rules, supporting various comparison types like equals, contains, and numerical comparisons. This expansion can be downloaded using `/papi ecloud download changeoutput`.

```APIDOC
%changeoutput_<options>_input:<input>_matcher:<matcher>_ifmatch:<output-if-matched>_else:<output-if-not-matched>%

<options>:
  equals - match the input exactly
  ignorecase - match the input while ignoring cases
  ignorecolor - match the input while ignoring colour codes
  contains - check if the match contains input
  >= - check if the input is larger than or equal to the matcher
  > - check if the input is larger than the matcher
  <= - check if the input is less than or equal to the matcher
  < - check if the input is less than the matcher
<input> - this is your text that you wish to replace
```

### CooldownBar PlaceholderAPI Placeholders

Source: https://github.com/placeholderapi/placeholderapi/wiki/temp_wiki_06579f44c797ae903e1c8d72358bb1c9bdeca778/Placeholders

### PlaceholderAPI Progress Bar Expansion

Source: https://github.com/placeholderapi/placeholderapi/wiki/Placeholders

Generates customizable progress bars based on a given placeholder's numerical value. Supports various symbols, lengths, and custom completion text for visual representation of progress.

```APIDOC
Download Command:
/papi ecloud download progress

Usage:
%progress_bar_{placeholder}%
  - Basic progress bar using default symbols and length.
%progress_bar_{placeholder}_c:<completed Symbol>%
  - Customize the symbol for the completed portion of the bar.
%progress_bar_{placeholder}_p:<progress Symbol>%
  - Customize the symbol for the current progress point.
%progress_bar_{placeholder}_r:<remaining Symbol>%
  - Customize the symbol for the remaining portion of the bar.
%progress_bar_{placeholder}_l:<max length>%
  - Set the maximum length of the progress bar.
%progress_bar_{placeholder}_m:<max value>%
  - Set the maximum value for the progress calculation (e.g., 100 for percentage).
%progress_bar_{placeholder}_fullbar:<text>%
  - Display custom text when the progress bar is 100% complete.

Example:
%progress_bar_{placeholder}_c:&a■_p:&e■_r:&7■_l:10_m:100_fullbar:&aCompleted!%
```

### Import PlaceholderAPI Dependency with Gradle

Source: https://github.com/placeholderapi/placeholderapi/wiki/Hook-into-PlaceholderAPI

Configure your "build.gradle" file to include the PlaceholderAPI repository and dependency for Gradle projects. Remember to replace "{VERSION}" with the target PlaceholderAPI version.

```Groovy
repositories {
    maven {
        url = 'https://repo.extendedclip.com/content/repositories/placeholderapi/'
    }
}

dependencies {
    compileOnly 'me.clip:placeholderapi:{VERSION}'
}
```

```APIDOC
%tokenenchant_<enchantment>_occurrence_<X>% : the activation chance (in %) for the <enchatnment> at the level <X>.  If <X> == 0, chance of the current level will be returned.
```

### PlaceholderAPI Armor Expansion

Source: https://github.com/placeholderapi/placeholderapi/wiki/temp_wiki_06579f44c797ae903e1c8d72358bb1c9bdeca778/Placeholders

Retrieves comprehensive information about a player's equipped armor. Users must specify the desired armor slot (helmet, chestplate, leggings, boots).

```PlaceholderAPI
Chose one value that's inside () and replace SLOT with one of the following: helmet, chestplate, leggings, boots.

%armor_amount_SLOT%
%armor_color_(red/green/blue/hex)_SLOT%
%armor_durability_(left/max)_SLOT%
%armor_has_SLOT%
%armor_material_SLOT%
%armor_maxamount_SLOT%
```

### ParseOther Expansion

Source: https://github.com/placeholderapi/placeholderapi/wiki/Placeholders

The ParseOther expansion enables parsing any PlaceholderAPI placeholder for a specified player. It supports parsing by username or UUID. For dynamic username/UUID resolution, 'unsafe' placeholders can be used, requiring curly braces `{}` around the player identifier.

```APIDOC
%parseother_{username}_{placeholder_without_percent_signs}%
  - Parses the specified placeholder for a player identified by their username.
  - Parameters:
    - {username}: Required. The exact username of the target player.
    - {placeholder_without_percent_signs}: Required. The placeholder to parse, without the leading and trailing percent signs.
%parseother_unsafe_{placeholder_for_username}_{placeholder_without_percent_signs}%
  - Parses the specified placeholder for a player whose username is resolved by another placeholder.
  - Parameters:
    - {placeholder_for_username}: Required. A placeholder that resolves to a player's username.
    - {placeholder_without_percent_signs}: Required. The placeholder to parse, without the leading and trailing percent signs.
%parseother_{uuid}_{placeholder_without_percent_signs}%
  - Parses the specified placeholder for a player identified by their UUID.
  - Parameters:
    - {uuid}: Required. The exact UUID of the target player.
    - {placeholder_without_percent_signs}: Required. The placeholder to parse, without the leading and trailing percent signs.
%parseother_unsafe_{placeholder_for_uuid}_{placeholder_without_percent_signs}%
  - Parses the specified placeholder for a player whose UUID is resolved by another placeholder.
  - Parameters:
    - {placeholder_for_uuid}: Required. A placeholder that resolves to a player's UUID.
    - {placeholder_without_percent_signs}: Required. The placeholder to parse, without the leading and trailing percent signs.
```

### PlaceholderAPI ChangeOutput Conditional Logic

Source: https://github.com/placeholderapi/placeholderapi/wiki/temp_wiki_06579f44c797ae903e1c8d72358bb1c9bdeca778/Placeholders

Allows dynamic modification of placeholder output based on conditional matching of an input value against a matcher. This powerful expansion supports various comparison operators and options for case-insensitivity, color code ignoring, and substring matching, providing flexible conditional logic.

```APIDOC
%changeoutput_<options>_input:<input>_matcher:<matcher>_ifmatch:<output-if-matched>_else:<output-if-not-matched>%
  - <options>: Controls the matching behavior. Multiple options can be combined.
    - equals: Match the input exactly.
    - ignorecase: Match the input while ignoring cases.
    - ignorecolor: Match the input while ignoring color codes.
    - contains: Check if the matcher contains the input.
    - >=: Check if the input is larger than or equal to the matcher.
    - >: Check if the input is larger than the matcher.
    - <=: Check if the input is less than or equal to the matcher.
    - <: Check if the input is less than the matcher.
  - <input>: The text or placeholder value to be evaluated against the matcher.
  - <matcher>: The value to compare against the input.
  - <output-if-matched>: The string to output if the condition is met.
  - <output-if-not-matched>: The string to output if the condition is not met.
```

--------------------------------

### PlaceholderAPI NumberFormatter Expansion Usage

Source: https://github.com/placeholderapi/placeholderapi/wiki/temp_wiki_06579f44c797ae903e1c8d72358bb1c9bdeca778/Placeholders

Documents the NumberFormatter expansion for PlaceholderAPI, which allows formatting numerical placeholders. It explains the syntax for applying various number formats, including locale-specific formatting, to existing numerical placeholders.

```APIDOC
%nf_<format>[:locale]_<other_placeholder>% # Converts a number produced by %other_placeholder% to a number using the specified format.
```

### DeluxeMenus PlaceholderAPI Placeholders

PlaceholderAPI placeholders for the DeluxeMenus plugin, used to retrieve metadata values with specified data types and default values, handling potential type mismatches.

```APIDOC
%deluxemenus_meta_<key>_<dataType>_<default_value>%

- Key: The key of the meta you want to check
- Data Type: Can be `STRING`, `BOOLEAN`, `DOUBLE`, `LONG`, or `INTEGER`.
  *If the given key has a different data type, an error will occur.*
- Default Value: The value returned if nothing is found.
```

### CraftConomy Plugin Placeholders

Source: https://github.com/placeholderapi/placeholderapi/wiki/Placeholders

Placeholders for the CraftConomy plugin, used to display currency values with options for multiplication and integer conversion. Useful for plugins requiring specific data types.

```APIDOC
%craftconomy_<currency>[option]%

Options:
- `_@<number>`
  Return `<currency>` times `<number>`. I.e. `%craftconomy_currency_@10%` returns `100` for `10 currency`
- `_@int`
  Return the number as Integer instead of double. Useful for plugins like DeluxeMenus that require an integer.
```

### Override canRegister for Plugin Instance Access in Java

Source: https://github.com/placeholderapi/placeholderapi/wiki/PlaceholderExpansion

This Java snippet illustrates how to override the `canRegister()` method within a PlaceholderAPI expansion to obtain a direct instance of a required Bukkit plugin. By casting the plugin retrieved from `PluginManager`, the expansion gains access to the dependent plugin's API or configuration. While functional, using a dedicated API provided by the dependent plugin is generally recommended over this direct access approach.

```Java
private SomePlugin plugin = null; // This would be the plugin your expansion depends on

@Override
public boolean canregister() {
    // This sets plugin to the SomePlugin instance you get through the PluginManager
    return (plugin = (SomePlugin) Bukkit.getPluginManager().getPlugin(getRequiredPlugin())) != null;
}
```

### PlayerStats PlaceholderAPI Placeholders

Source: https://github.com/placeholderapi/placeholderapi/wiki/Placeholders

Comprehensive placeholders for retrieving player and server statistics. Supports various statistics types (item/block, entity), top player lists, and detailed player-specific data. Allows filtering for raw numbers, formatted numbers, or player names. Requires the PlayerStatsExpansion.

```PlaceholderAPI
%playerstats_prefix% 
%playerstats_prefixtitle% 
%playerstats_rainbowprefix% 
%playerstats_rainbowprefixtitle% 
%playerstats_top:<n>,<statistic>%

%playerstats_top:<n>,<statistic>,only:number% 
%playerstats_top:<n>,<statistic>,only:number_raw%
%playerstats_top:<n>,<statistic>,only:player_name% 
%playerstats_player:<player-name>,<statistic>% 
%playerstats_player:<player-name>,<statistic:item/block>% 
%playerstats_player:<player-name>,<statistic:entity>% 
%playerstats_player:<player-name>,<statistic>,only:number% 
%playerstats_player:<player-name>,<statistic>,only:number_raw% 
%playerstats_me,<statistic>% 
%playerstats_me,<statistic:item/block>% 
%playerstats_me,<statistic:entity>% 
%playerstats_me,<statistic>,only:number% 
%playerstats_me,<statistic>,only:number_raw% 
%playerstats_server,<statistic>% 
%playerstats_server,<statistic:item/block>% 
%playerstats_server,<statistic:entity>% 
%playerstats_server,<statistic>,only:number% 
%playerstats_server,<statistic>,only:number_raw% 
%playerstats_title,<statistic>% 
%playerstats_title:[n],<statistic>%

%playerstats_title:[n],<statistic:entity>%
```

Documents the ParseOther expansion for PlaceholderAPI, designed to parse any placeholder for a specific other player. It highlights the use of unsafe placeholders for username or uuid and emphasizes the requirement for curly braces in the syntax.

```APIDOC

%thetime_date_minute% - Current minute
%thetime_date_hour% - Current hour
%thetime_date_day% - Current day of month
%thetime_date_day_name% - Current day name
%thetime_date_dayOfWeek% - Current day of week
%thetime_date_week% - Current week of year
%thetime_date_month% - Current month number
%thetime_date_month_name% - Current month name
%thetime_date_year% - Current year
%thetime_date_era% - Current era
%thetime_date_era_name% - Current era name
```

Source: https://github.com/placeholderapi/placeholderapi/wiki/temp_wiki_06579f44c797ae903e1c8d72358bb1c9bdeca778/Placeholders
